# Open data sources of Bratislava (data.bratislava.sk)

Which items of the Bratislava open data hub (`data.bratislava.sk`, an ArcGIS Hub site) the
`bratislava` project takes, in which batch, as which Smart Data Model, and which it leaves out with
the reason. It follows [Open data integration rules](../Development/12-open-data-integration.md) and
has the shape of [Open data sources of Helsinki (HRI)](helsinki-open-data.md). The owner orders of
2026-10-22 ask for the usable open data of four cities as NGSI-LD Smart Data Models, validated,
useful datasets first, at least 100 per city. Task T-5272 built this page; T-5273 (live data) and
T-5274 (the rest) build the pipelines.

## 1. How the matrix was made

1. **Snapshot.** The hub's OGC Records search API
   (`/api/search/v1/collections/dataset/items`) lists 673 items: 671 ArcGIS Feature Services on
   `services8.arcgis.com` (organisation `pRlN1m0su5BYaFAS`), the MHD timetable as a GTFS zip and the
   land-use plan as a Shapefile zip. The API takes no sort order, so paging with `startindex`
   returns some items twice and others never. The snapshot was read in slices of the `created` date
   (`filter=created >= a AND created < b`) small enough to fit one page each; it holds 673 distinct
   item ids and 671 distinct service URLs. A copy is in
   `Research/data/bratislava-hub-snapshot-2026-10-23.tsv`.
2. **The supervisor's inventory was incomplete.** The list
   `evidence/supervisor-2026-10-22-bratislava-inventory/bratislava-datasets.tsv` that the task
   names was read with `startindex` paging: of its 673 lines only 497 name distinct services, and
   176 hub items are missing from it (among them the pedestrian and cyclist counters). This page
   uses the snapshot of step 1 instead.
3. **Probe.** For each Feature Service on 2026-10-23 between 04:00Z and 04:10Z: the service
   description (`?f=json`), its first layer or table (geometry type, field names,
   `editingInfo.lastEditDate`) and the record count (`query?where=1=1&returnCountOnly=true`), with
   a 20 s timeout and a 2 MB read cap. Every service answered. 656 are attribute tables, 11 hold
   polygons and 4 points. The `last edit` column is `lastEditDate`, the hub's own `modified` stamp
   changes with every metadata touch and says nothing about the data.
4. **Licences.** 659 items are CC BY 4.0. The 13 ledgers of invoices, orders and contracts are
   CC BY-NC 4.0: their indicators carry that licence and the catalogue entry says so. The
   land-use plan Shapefile says CC BY 4.0 in the search API but CC BY-NC-ND 4.0 in its own
   licence text; the stricter text decides, so it is skipped (no derivatives).
5. **Judgement.** Entity rows (points, polygons, tables with coordinates or addresses, lists of
   places) were read by hand: field names and one sample record each. Statistics rows follow the
   rules of sections 2 and 6.1.

## 2. Decisions

- **integrate**: the item becomes NGSI-LD entities of one Smart Data Model (or a custom model that
  borrows the members of the named closest SDM class) through a pipeline of the named batch.
- **statistics**: an attribute table of figures becomes `KeyPerformanceIndicator` series, one
  indicator per table and dimension cut, through one Feature Service table pipeline (one pipeline
  shape for all 583 tables, configured per table, never one pipeline per file).
- **skip**: nothing is built; the reason is in the row and in section 7.

Items of one series (yearly or per-snapshot copies of the same table or list) are one dataset with
history: the `note` names the series and the batch loads the items as time slices of one dataset.
After that collapse the 637 integrate and statistics rows are 602 datasets.

| batch | domain | integrate | statistics | rows |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T-5273 | live counters, MHD timetable, bike share, coach stops; traffic, accident, transit, cycling, bridge and air statistics | 9 | 27 | 36 |
| T-5274 | districts, places and services, trees and greenery, climate grid, issue reports; every other statistics table | 45 | 556 | 601 |
| - | skip | | | 36 |
| total | | 54 | 583 | 673 |

Two sources outside the hub belong to T-5273: the visitbratislava events that the `slovensko`
seed already reads (`slovensko-pipeline-podujatia-visitbratislava*.yaml` in the deployment
repository) move to the `bratislava` space as `Event`, and the live MHD vehicle positions and the
Slovnaft BAjk availability (GBFS), which the hub does not publish, are T-5273's to find or to list
as a gap. The hub has no parking occupancy and no air-quality sensor feed; the air rows here are
yearly statistics.

## 3. Smart Data Models pinned

Every model name in the matrix is a directory of the subject repository at
`https://github.com/smart-data-models/dataModel.<subject>`, at the commits the Helsinki page pins
(read on 2026-10-22), so the four city batches validate against the same schemas.
`CrowdFlowObserved`, `GtfsRoute` and `GtfsTrip` were checked at those commits on 2026-10-23.

| subject | commit | classes used |
|---|---|---|
| `Building` | `92aca4467c` | `Building` |
| `IssueTracking` | `37cd76540e` | `IssueReporting` |
| `KeyPerformanceIndicator` | `ec7f3b642f` | `KeyPerformanceIndicator` |
| `Parking` | `bac46424de` | `OnStreetParking` |
| `ParksAndGardens` | `88223ee898` | `FlowerBed`, `GreenspaceRecord` |
| `PointOfInterest` | `7e5e24963a` | `PointOfInterest` |
| `Transportation` | `b83ede9d3d` | `BikeHireDockingStation`, `CityWork`, `CrowdFlowObserved`, `Road`, `TrafficFlowObserved` |
| `UrbanMobility` | `81a052c84d` | `GtfsRoute`, `GtfsStop`, `GtfsTrip` |

Custom models exist only where no subject has a class for the thing, and they are the ones the
Helsinki page defines: `AdministrativeArea` (city districts), `Tree` (closest `FlowerBed`), `Street`
(closest `Road`), `LandParcel` (closest `PointOfInterest`). One is new: `ClimateRiskArea` (closest
`FloodMonitoring`), one 200 m cell of the city's climate atlas with every risk and sensitivity
layer as an attribute.

## 4. Outputs

The `outputs` column names the representations the batch configures on the dataset's Endpoint,
following the [representation contract](../API/11-representation-contract.md):

- `ngsi-ld`: the broker view, always.
- `temporal`: the NGSI-LD history, where values change over time (counters, issue status,
  indicator series).
- `geojson` and `ogc-features`: where the entities carry a geometry.
- `csv`: points and tables a spreadsheet user wants.

No hub item has events with a start time or items that appear over time as news, so no row asks
for `ical` or `feed`; the visitbratislava events of T-5273 get both.

## 5. Checks every batch runs

The owner order of 2026-10-22 18:10Z adds plausibility checks beside the SDM schema check. Each
batch task runs them on the real source data and writes the result into its own body:

- **Location**: every point or area inside the `AdministrativeArea` of Bratislava (the union of
  the 17 districts of row `Mestská časť`) plus 1 km; no 0,0; no swapped latitude and longitude.
- **Values**: physical ranges per property (counts at least 0, percentages 0 to 100, occupancy at
  most capacity, speeds 0 to 150 km/h) and the units the SDM names; dates no more than 1 day in
  the future and not older than the source claims.
- **Identity**: no two entities for one real object (same id, or same name within 20 m); every
  reference (`refDevice`, district links) resolves to an existing entity.
- **Completeness**: the entity count matches the Feature Service `returnCountOnly` count (within
  1 % for live feeds); a source that returns 0 records or data older than 3 update periods fails
  the run visibly and deletes nothing. Feature Services page with `resultOffset` and
  `resultRecordCount` (at most 2,000 per request) up to a record cap per dataset.
- **Human read**: 5 random entities per dataset compared with the hub page or map, with the link.

The probe found coordinate traps a mapping must handle and a fixture must hold:

- `Databáza poskytovateľov celoživotného vzdelávania`: the field `X` holds the latitude and `Y`
  the longitude (sample `X` 48.145, `Y` 17.160).
- `Bezbariérovosť v meste Bratislava`: `X` and `Y` are strings with a decimal comma (`17,10997746`).
- `Starostlivosť o dreviny v Bratislave`: `X` and `Y` are S-JTSK (EPSG:5514, `-571717.9`,
  `-1280430.7`); the layer geometry queried with `outSR=4326` gives WGS 84.
- `Čierne skládky v meste Bratislava v roku 2025`: one string `"017.10675631 48.09139812"`,
  longitude first with a leading zero.
- `Práca s podnetmi zadanými Magistrátu`: the longitude field is spelt `LONGTITUDE`.
- Lists of places without coordinates (schools, sports grounds, fountains, cooling places,
  monuments, social services, city offices) are geocoded by address against the national address
  register first and Nominatim second; a row that does not resolve inside the city is listed, not
  placed at a guess.

Each pipeline fixture holds one out-of-area point, one out-of-range value and one duplicate, and the
pipeline drops or flags each with a counted reason.

## 6. The matrix

### 6.1 Usefulness score and the 100 target

The owner order of 2026-10-22 18:25Z asks the batches to build the useful rows first. Each
integrate and statistics row scores one point for each of:

- **live**: the source updates at least monthly (the counters hourly, the timetable with each
  change, tree care and the waste and funeral-service ledgers within the last month);
- **city question**: it answers a citizen or city question about Bratislava: traffic, transit,
  cycling, air, waste and water, places and services, schools, safety, health, population and
  housing. Rows about the hub's own tools (Free WiFi telemetry, technical-map orders, police and
  MHD fleet age, utility finances) or comparing other regions and countries score no point;
- **joins**: it joins entities the space holds or another row adds: a breakdown by city district,
  district (okres), basic settlement unit (ZSJ) or cadastral area, counters, stops, schools;

and loses one point when it is an invoice, order, contract, grant or budget ledger, or an archive
whose last period ended before 2021 (SODB 2011, series to 2020 and older). A row scoring 1 or more
is **useful**; a batch builds its useful rows highest score first and leaves the rest listed. A
skip row has no score.

| batch | useful rows | not useful rows | useful datasets |
|---|---|---|---|
| T-5273 | 32 | 4 | 31 |
| T-5274 | 473 | 128 | 455 |

The owner order of 18:30Z sets a floor of 100 live Smart Data Models datasets per city (counted by
T-5275). Bratislava has 38 useful entity datasets and 448 useful statistics datasets, so the floor
is reached from useful rows alone. On 2026-10-25 none of them was live on dev: section 6.5
re-scores every row and plans the rest. The entity side is thin: the hub publishes
few places with coordinates: 18 of the 38 entity datasets need geocoding by address, and two (streets,
parcels) carry no geometry at all.

### 6.2 All rows

`item` links the hub page of the item id. `source` is the Feature Service kind or the file.
`last edit` is the layer's `lastEditDate`. `records` is the Feature Service record count on
2026-10-23 (`file` for the two zip files). `score` is the usefulness of section 6.1. `usable`,
`dev` and `plan` are the re-score of 2026-10-25 (section 6.5): `yes` or the rule the row fails,
`seeded, 404` when its Endpoint is in the deployment seed and does not answer on dev, and the
batch that makes it live. Rows are
ordered by batch, integrate before statistics, then by score and title.

### 6.3 Batch T-5273: what was built

T-5273 (deployment repository, `seed/bratislava/`) built the counters, the shared bikes and the
public transport stops and lines from DPB's GTFS; each
dataset is its own Endpoint, public Policy and CKAN dataset in the organisation `bratislava`. The
rows it did not build carry their reason here; their work is task T-5435.

| # | title | state | Endpoint or reason |
|---|---|---|---|

The visitbratislava events stay in the `slovensko` space until a move is shown not to change the
Slovakia event counts (T-5365); T-5435. DPB publishes no open GTFS-realtime feed on the hub.

### 6.4 Batch T-5274: what was built

T-5274 (deployment repository, `seed/bratislava/`) built three pipeline shapes:
`districts` (the 17 city districts as `AdministrativeArea`), `places` (four tables of places with
coordinates as `PointOfInterest`) and the one Feature Service table shape for statistics as
`KeyPerformanceIndicator`, run by three pipelines split by area so that a run fits its memory
quota: `statistics` (the 17 city districts, 15,765 figures on 2026-10-24, 320 MiB quota),
`statistics-okresy` (Bratislava I to V, 10,257 figures, 256 MiB) and `statistics-mesto` (the city
as a whole, 10,231 figures, 256 MiB); all three run the same mapping text, a table is one line of
its `tables`. A dataset name that would end in a year takes the suffix `-ba`: the Portal's
residue janitor may remove names ending in four digits (T-2627). Every built row is its own
Endpoint, public Policy (narrowed by `idPattern` to its table's ids) and CKAN dataset in the organisation `bratislava`: 332 datasets, 36,253 figures.

How the statistics rows were sorted, on 2026-10-24: each of the batch's 601 Feature Services was
read once whole (`/0?f=json` for its fields, `/0/query?where=1=1&outFields=*` for at most 2,000
rows). A table is built when it has an area column naming at least 10 of the 17 city districts or
3 of the 5 okresy (or no area column and a title about Bratislava: the city as a whole), a period
column of years, ranges or quarters (or a year or range in its title), at least one column whose
values are numbers in 80 % of the rows, at most two breakdown columns (age, sex, category), and
fewer than 20 % repeated rows. A table of Bratislava beside Slovakia keeps the Bratislava rows
(`only`); a figure outside its bounds (counts 0 or more, shares 0 to 100) or a row outside the
table's areas is dropped and counted. The mapping and an independent reading of every recorded
table give the same ids and values (tests/test_bratislava_hub.py); every figure validates against
the space's schema and the vendored SDM `KeyPerformanceIndicator` schema.

Score 0 and -1 rows of the same shape are listed, not loaded: section 6.1 builds the useful rows.
Source defects the run counts instead of hiding: "Zmena počtu obyvateľov v mestských častiach
Bratislavy medzi rokom 2022 a 2035" lists one district twice (the first is kept); several tables write `None` in place of a number. "Počet evidovaných vodičov" writes 13 of its 50 years as fractions ("2018,636364") and six police tables of the okresy write their third period as a half year ("01/2018 - 06/2018"): those rows are dropped as "no period". The two population-pyramid tables ("Veková štruktúra obyvateľov Bratislavy v roku 2021", "Vekové zloženie obyvateľov Bratislavy a Slovenska v roku 2021") write the men as negative numbers for the chart: dropped as "out of range" (a count is 0 or more), the women kept. "Spôsoby riešenia priestupkov" writes "-" for none: no number.

### 6.5 Re-score of 2026-10-25 and the plan to 100

The owner order of 2026-10-25 14:20Z asks for at least 100 usable Bratislava datasets readable on
their NGSI-LD Endpoints, nothing unusable integrated, and a read back through the MCP connector.
T-5664 re-read the hub and dev on 2026-10-25 and filled the `usable`, `dev` and `plan` columns of
section 6.2.

**Hub re-read.** The search API listed 673 items at 14:40Z, read in `created` slices as in
section 1, the same 673 ids as on 2026-10-23. Every Feature Service answered its description,
layer and count query (20 s timeout, 2 MB cap) and both zip files answered; no layer is empty.
Eight items changed: the three counter tables (to 2026-10-25), tree care, the contracts and
orders ledgers and the two zip files. The copy is
`Research/data/bratislava-hub-snapshot-2026-10-25.tsv`, with a `status` column (`ok`, `dead`,
`empty`).

**Rule.** A row is usable when all of these hold, else its column names the first rule it fails:

| word | rule | rows |
|---|---|---|
| `dead` | the source answers today | 0 |
| `empty` | the layer holds rows and at least one figure; nine tables write `None` in every figure cell | 12 |
| `pdf-only` | a machine-readable download or API (Feature Service, GTFS, CSV, GeoJSON) | 0 |
| `licence` | an open licence: CC BY-NC (13 ledgers) and CC BY-NC-ND (the land-use plan) are not open | 14 |
| `stale` | updated within its stated period, or a static set for the years in its title: the Covid monitoring, the tram departures of 2017-2019 and the issue reports, whose hub copy stopped in 2024-07 although it states a running log | 9 |
| `outside` | at least one row inside the city: figures of Slovakia, the BSK region, the regions of Slovakia, the BVS supply area, other countries or the Trnava region only | 42 |
| `duplicate` | not a copy or an older year of another row: the BAjk stations of 2024 (the live GBFS feed of `bratislava-bajk` replaces it) and the kindergartens of 2025 (row 49 is 2026) | 2 |
| `no-model` | the rows describe a thing a Smart Data Model or an indicator can hold: council records, survey answers, observation sheets, helplines, address-to-school lookups, record ledgers | 34 |
| `personal` | no personal data: one count study lists vehicle registration numbers | 1 |
| `yes` | usable | 559 |

The rule words `no-model`, `outside` and `personal` are added to the six the task names.
Decided (worker-t5664, owner may override): they keep a row out of the count, because a row the
broker cannot hold as an entity, a row about another area and a row with personal data are not
usable Bratislava data; options were to count them as usable and leave them unbuilt, or to fold
them into `empty`. Usefulness (the score of section 6.1) is not a usability rule: score 0 and -1
rows are usable and planned last.

**Dev on 2026-10-25 14:41Z.** All 338 Bratislava Endpoints of the deployment seed answer 404 on
`https://api.dev.joinedcontext.com/api/endpoint/<slug>/ngsi-ld/v1/types`; Helsinki Endpoints
answer 200 on the same path. They serve 336 usable hub rows (`seeded, 404`) plus `bratislava-bajk`
(GBFS) and the second GTFS Endpoint. The cause is one for every row: the Portal publishes none of
the `bratislava` space's Endpoints, although the dev forge holds all of them (task T-5648, its
supervisor section of 14:40Z). No row is live with data.

| state | usable rows |
|---|---|
| live with data on dev | 0 |
| seeded, Endpoint answers 404 | 336 |
| not seeded | 223 |
| total usable | 559 |

**Plan to 100.** 559 rows are usable, so 100 is reached from usable rows alone; the gap today is
100, because none is live. The fix of T-5648 makes up to 336 rows live at once, with no new
pipeline: it closes the gap, and 3 of its datasets then read back through the MCP connector. The
223 usable rows not seeded go to the two parked batches, which are kept, and ten new batches of at
most 25 rows, one pipeline shape each. Every statistics row stays a `KeyPerformanceIndicator`
through the one Feature Service table pipeline; a batch adds only what its shape needs.

### 6.6 Batch T-5671: what was built

P1 found that 16 of its 20 tables are ledgers with one row per event or booking (police events,
fines, Free Wifi days, DTMB authorisations and deposits, budget lines, school transfers), and
that 4 of them answer at most 1,000 rows a query (`maxRecordCount`). Paging alone would write no
figure for a ledger, so the one table pipeline does two things more, still one mapping text for
the three statistics pipelines:

- Every table is read in pages of 1,000 rows with `resultOffset`, until the hub stops saying
  `exceededTransferLimit`, at most 20 pages; a table with more, or a page the hub refuses, writes
  nothing of itself and the others still write.
- A table line with `group`, `stats` and `where` asks the hub for its own counts and sums per
  group (`groupByFieldsForStatistics`, `outStatistics`, ordered by the group so the pages do not
  overlap). The year comes from a SUBSTRING of the date text; `labels` turns a code into its
  word (budget `P`/`V` as príjmy/výdavky); `past` drops a period that starts after today.

| row | table | pages | groups | hub rows counted | how |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 150 | offences by event 2017-2022 | 2 | 1,837 | 342,446 of 342,446 | count per year, group of event and district |

Read on 2026-10-25. Row 237 moved to P5: its 50,661 rows are the 1 km census grid of the whole
of Slovakia, and only P5 builds the grid as an area. The grouped sums match a sum of the
downloaded rows (row 609, expenditure class 63 of 2021: 534 lines, 83,770,477.40 EUR both ways).

## 8. Provenance

- Hub items: `https://data.bratislava.sk/api/search/v1/collections/dataset/items`, read on
  2026-10-23 at 04:00Z in `created` slices (section 1), copied to
  `Research/data/bratislava-hub-snapshot-2026-10-23.tsv` with the probe's record count, geometry
  type and last edit per item.
- Feature Services: `https://services8.arcgis.com/pRlN1m0su5BYaFAS/arcgis/rest/services/<name>/FeatureServer`,
  each read once on 2026-10-23 between 04:00Z and 04:10Z (section 1, step 3).
- The two files: `https://www.arcgis.com/sharing/rest/content/items/<id>/data` (GTFS 6.2 MB,
  modified 2026-10-22; Shapefile 4.8 MB, licence text CC BY-NC-ND 4.0).
- Smart Data Models: the GitHub API listing of each subject repository at the commit in section 3.
- Re-read on 2026-10-25 at 14:40Z (section 6.5): the same search API and probe, copied to
  `Research/data/bratislava-hub-snapshot-2026-10-25.tsv`; the dev Endpoints at 14:41Z, one
  `GET /api/endpoint/<slug>/ngsi-ld/v1/types` per seeded slug of the deployment repository
  (`components/context-gateway/seed/bratislava/`, commit `beb4b5b3`).
- `scripts/check-bratislava-open-data.py probe` re-reads the count query of 10 sampled usable rows
  and fails on a dead one.
- `scripts/check-bratislava-open-data.py check` proves the counts: 673 rows, one per snapshot id,
  licence and record count equal to the snapshot, every integrate or statistics row with a batch of
  T-5273 or T-5274 and a model, every skip in section 7.
